Price-snapshot store — opening and closing numbers

One row per market (SIDE / TOTAL / MONEYLINE) for every 2026 REG game we originate or reference, with the opening number, the closing number, their capture times, and settlement. Where no verified opening capture exists (weeks 1–9, 2026) the open cells are blank and flagged — never filled in. Market prices are third-party by nature (the NFL publishes no odds); settlements are verified against official scores.

Example (the store doing its job): WAS @ NYG, week 10 — SIDE open 2.5 (pre-game capture) → close 1.5 (settled archive). The older A-06 flag treated 1.5 vs 2.5 as a discrepancy; the store shows it as open-vs-close line movement with both sources recorded.
